            "proposal": "1) New file voicetwin/utils/progress.py, with no gradio import so the CLI and tests can use it. `class ProgressTracker` takes `stages: List[Tuple[float, str, str]]` = (start fraction, Chinese stage name, unit). Calling it as `__call__(frac, msg)` matches the existing ProgressFn signature. Under a threading.Lock it: keeps frac monotonic (`max(old, new)`); finds the stage index from the fraction; resets stage_t0 when the stage changes; parses step/total from msg with `re.search(r\"(\\d+)\\s*/\\s*(\\d+)\", msg)`, since messages already contain '[i/N]', '识别 i/N', '计算声纹 i/N', '评估 X：i/N'; and stores last_update. `finish(ok, msg)` sets status to done or error and, on success, frac to 1.0. `snapshot()` returns a plain dict under the lock and computes the time estimate there: p = step/total if a total is known, else (frac-lo)/(hi-lo) within the stage. The stage's remaining time is stage_elapsed*(1-p)/p, shown only when p≥0.05 and stage_elapsed≥20 s and smoothed as an EMA (0.7 old / 0.3 new); otherwise the text is '正在估算……'. Durations are formatted as '不到 1 分钟' / '约 N 分钟' / '约 1 小时 20 分'. 2) `render_progress_html(snap)` HTML-escapes msg with html.escape, because transcripts can contain '<'. 'CUDA out of memory|OutOfMemoryError|CUBLAS_STATUS_ALLOC_FAILED' → '显卡内存（显存）不够' / '关掉游戏、剪映、在线视频等占用显卡的程序，再点一次；训练时可以打开「② 训练模型」的「高级设置」，把「每批数量」改成 2；生成时把质量改成「快速试听」。'\n2. 'MemoryError|Unable to allocate|bad allocation' → '电脑内存不够' / '关掉其他程序再试；如果视频超过 2 小时，先剪成 1 小时以内的几段。'\n3. 'No space left|Errno 28|WinError 112' → '硬盘空间不够' / '请清理 D 盘和 C 盘，至少空出 30 GB 再试。'\n4. 'WinError 32|being used by another process|Errno 13|Permission denied|PermissionError' → '文件被别的程序占用' / '如果用 Excel/WPS 打开了 transcripts.csv，或者用播放器打开了音频，请先关掉再试。'\n5. 'no CUDA-capable device|CUDA driver version is insufficient|Torch not compiled with CUDA|NVIDIA-SMI has failed|NVIDIA driver' → '显卡驱动没装好或太旧' / '到 https://www.nvidia.cn/drivers/lookup/ 下载安装最新驱动，然后重启电脑。'\n6. 'no kernel image is available' → '显卡太新，这个整合包不支持' / 'RTX 50 系列请使用 nvidia50 版的 GPT-SoVITS 整合包。'\n7. 'HTTPSConnectionPool|Max retries exceeded|ConnectionError|ConnectTimeout|ReadTimeout|timed out|LocalEntryNotFoundError|getaddrinfo|SSLError|ProxyError|预训练模型下载失败' → '下载失败（网络问题）' / '检查网络后再点一次；如果开着 VPN 或加速器，先关掉再试；第一次使用需要联网下载模型。'\n8. 'does not contain any stream' → '这个视频里没有声音' / '换一个有声音的视频，或者把它从文件夹里拿走。'\n9. 'Invalid data found|moov atom not found|ffmpeg 执行失败' → '视频文件损坏或没复制完整' / '重新复制这个视频，或用剪映重新导出一次。'\n10. '找不到 GPT-SoVITS 目录|不是完整的 GPT-SoVITS 目录' → '没找到 GPT-SoVITS 整合包' / '重新双击 install_windows.bat，输入整合包的位置（例如 D:\\GPT-SoVITS）。'\n11. '缺少预训练模型' → '缺少 GPT-SoVITS 的模型文件' / '到「🩺 环境检查」页点「⬇️ 下载缺少的模型」。'\n12. '还没有名为|还没有参考音频|没有可用于评估的片段|没有验证集' → '这个声音还没有准备素材' / '先在「① 准备素材」里点「开始准备素材」。'\n13. '没有找到任何视频或音频文件|找不到文件夹' → '文件夹里没找到视频或录音' / '检查文件夹路径是否写对（可以在文件夹窗口顶部的地址栏复制路径，再粘贴过来）。'\n14. '讲稿里没有可以朗读的内容' → '讲稿是空的' / '在「讲稿」框里粘贴文字，或者上传 txt / Word 文件。'\n15. '推理服务启动失败|推理服务启动超时' → 'GPT-SoVITS 没能启动' / '关掉所有声音分身的黑色窗口后重新打开再试；还不行就到「🩺 环境检查」页看看。'\n16.       "interfaces": "A. Progress callback (unchanged): ProgressFn = Callable[[float, str], None].\n- frac is in [0, 1]; msg is simple Chinese.\n- Callers may send any frac. The tracker clamps it and keeps it monotonic (it only moves forward).\n- Every backend call to a progress callback is wrapped in try/except Exception. TaskCancelled is a BaseException and must pass through.\n\nB. voicetwin/utils/progress.py (U1, no gradio import, Python 3.9 typing)\n- Stage = Tuple[float, str], meaning (start frac, Chinese step name), in ascending order.\n- class TaskCancelled(BaseException)\n- CANCEL: threading.Event; request_cancel(); clear_cancel(); check_cancel() raises TaskCancelled when set.\n- class ProgressTracker:\n  - __init__(stages: Optional[Sequence[Stage]] = None, title: str = '', hint: str = '', clock: Callable[[], float] = time.time, on_update: Optional[Callable[[Dict[str, Any]], None]] = None)\n  - __call__(frac: float, msg: str = '') -> None. Thread-safe. Calls check_cancel() first. Never raises anything except TaskCancelled.\n  - finish(ok: bool, msg: str = '', stopped: bool = False) -> None\n  - snapshot() -> Dict[str, Any] with keys:\n    - status: 'running' | 'done' | 'error' | 'stopped'\n    - frac: float\n    - pct: int, 0-100\n    - title\n    - stage_index: int, 1-based; 0 means no stages\n    - stage_count\n    - stage_name\n    - msg\n    - step: Optional[int]\n    - total: Optional[int]\n    - elapsed: seconds\n    - stage_elapsed: seconds\n    - eta: Optional[float], seconds left in the current stage\n    - eta_text: str\n    - idle: seconds since the last update\n    - hint\n    - run_id: str\n    - error: str\n- format_duration(s) -> str; format_elapsed(s) -> str\n- render_progress_html(snap, note: str = '') -> str. After that it yields every POLL_SECONDS. A single yield; text is the Chinese refusal message and bar is a warning notice.\n    - attached: True, when it followed an already-running task of the same kind and voice\n    - done\n  - Only one task runs at a time, guarded by a module-level lock. The log handler and the line buffer belong to the worker thread, so refreshing or closing the page does not stop the work.\n- current_task() -> Optional[Dict] with keys kind, label, voice, running, started, finished, ok, snap.\n- task_banner_md() -> str.
